Total turns parsed: 5999

================================================================================
COLUMN STRUCTURE:
================================================================================
- Turn number: Sequential turn identifier
- Game number: Which game this turn belongs to
- Roll 1-6: Individual roll values (dice rolls)
- Score: Sum of all rolls in that turn
